# Household hh_s8 — 42 days, Monday to Sunday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: couple. Rooms: bathroom, bedroom_1, entry, kitchen, living, storage.

Residents:

- **Felix** (resident_1): a student with classes out; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.53); punctual (jitter scale 0.74); rarely forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.84); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: board_games, cycling, family_calls, reading_living. Chores they take on: fixing, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.
- **Aisha** (resident_2): retired; very tidy (tidiness 0.92); punctual (jitter scale 0.76); rarely forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.57); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: cycling, gaming, photography. Chores they take on: fixing, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.

## Day 10 — Thursday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d10`: Aisha is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:38–16:08).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_1:d10`: Felix gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:23–21:08).

## Day 18 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:sore_back:resident_2:d18`: Aisha has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Felix): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Aisha): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `distracted:resident_1`: Felix is distracted (0.78): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Felix has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Aisha has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

Internal states (0–1): Felix energy 0.00, hurriedness 0.13, distraction 0.78; Aisha energy 0.00, hurriedness 0.00, distraction 0.28

## Day 25 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d25`: Aisha is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:10–15:40).
- `ep:new_game_release:resident_2:d24:next_morning`: Aisha is tired after a late night of gaming: a slower, shorter morning (06:30–12:00).
- `ep:overslept:resident_1:d25`: Felix overslept: a rushed morning with things dumped at the door, then a normal day (06:30–10:30).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Felix): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Aisha): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Felix has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Aisha has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 28 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_2:d28`: Aisha gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:16–21:01).
- `ep:new_game_release:resident_2:d28:tonight`: Aisha has a new game: the evening is gaming, and the controller, headset and glass stay by the couch (19:53–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Felix): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Aisha): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Felix has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Aisha has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
